1|# Morning priority refresh — Qu statement-domain correction v2
2|
3|Recorded 2026-10-21T09:50:36-0700 after `/bin/date`. Applies after morning erratum v1 and the clock qualification in [parent-record addendum v5](/Users/jinleic/repos/aifours/institute/runs/six-lane-2026-10-18-96h/report/recorded-deviations-addendum-v5-parent-records.md); original note bytes remain unchanged.
4|
5|The morning note's 2610.26122 statement must include **finite NON-CYCLIC G**, with p the smallest prime divisor of |G|. In [Qu 2610.26122v1, Corollary1.5](https://arxiv.org/html/2610.26122v1#S1.Thmtheorem5), the author claims `E(G) ≤ |G| + |G|/p + p − 2`, equality iff a cyclic subgroup of index p exists. The stronger [Theorem1.4](https://arxiv.org/html/2610.26122v1#S1.Thmtheorem4) claims `E(G) ≤ |G| + |G|/p − 1` when there is no such cyclic subgroup. Do not extend the corollary's domain to cyclic groups.
6|
7|The abstract and Introduction define sequences as finite multisets and allow selected terms to be **ordered** to give identity; this is the unordered Gao constant, not our increasing-original-index target. This increment directly read the rendered abstract/Introduction including both numbered statements (reader artifact `artifact://25024`, displayed LF1–112). It did not check the whole proof, publication status or novelty. Prior statements of a full-HTML reading are retained as earlier provenance, not newly certified by this bounded read. Attribute the theorems to the preprint rather than claim independent correctness certification. The comparison remains adjacent prior art, not a solution of ordered Olson.

99|- **CFR(r,n) ⇔ (Zₙ,r+1;1) difference matrix holds** under the standard definition:
100|  - Let gᵢ be the map from symbol to position in row i.
101|  - Rows i ≠ j conflict exactly when gᵢ − gⱼ is not a bijection.
102|  - Adding the row g₀ = 0 gives the difference matrix; normalizing a difference matrix by one row reverses this.
